The paradigm ***humiliatio + exaltatio*** analytical framework is paradigm-***Melanchthonian-Loci*** and paradigm-***Chemnitz-Loci-inherited***, and paradigm-***codified in FC SD VIII De persona Christi***. — The paradigm ***dextera-Dei-omnipotens*** framework — anti-Sacramentarian, cross-reference to Praemonitio §7 above (Christ's ubiquity per session at the omnipotent right-hand of God). Standard modern treatment: **Bruce L. McCormack,** ***For Us and Our Salvation: Incarnation and Atonement in the Reformed Tradition*** (Grand Rapids, 2015) — for paradigm-Reformed-parallel treatment of the two-states framework.

¹⁰⁷ʰ **The paradigm ***octuple-beneficia-Christi*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***catalogue-of-salvific-benefits***: (1) redemptio; (2) satisfactio; (3) reconciliatio; (4) iustificatio; (5) adoptio; (6) sanctificatio; (7) conservatio; (8) glorificatio — the paradigm-***classical Reformation-Lutheran ordo-salutis*** framework, inherited by paradigm ***Chemnitz Loci*** and paradigm ***Gerhard Loci*** IX-XII *De Christo-Iustificatione-Sanctificatione-Glorificatione*, and paradigm-***codified in FC SD III De iustificatione*** *(Kolb-Wengert 562-572)*. Selnecker's paradigm-***forward-cross-reference*** to **Pars II** — where the ***Iustificatio*** locus receives its full treatment — is significant: Selnecker deliberately deferred the fullest development of paradigm-***sola-fide-iustificatio*** to Pars II, treating here in Pars I only its Christological ground. Standard modern treatment: **Robert D. Preus,** ***Justification and Rome*** (St. Louis, 1997), pp. 45-110; **Michael Horton,** ***Justification*** (Grand Rapids, 2018).

¹⁰⁸ **De Spiritu Sancto** — the paradigm-Pneumatological Chapter. Standard modern treatment: **Anthony Thiselton,** ***The Holy Spirit — In Biblical Teaching, through the Centuries, and Today*** (Grand Rapids, 2013); **Michael Welker,** ***God the Spirit*** (Minneapolis, 1994); **Yves Congar,** ***I Believe in the Holy Spirit*** (New York, 1983, 3 vols.); **Denis Edwards,** ***Breath of Life: A Theology of the Creator Spirit*** (Maryknoll, 2004). Selnecker's chapter is a paradigm-***Person + Consubstantiality + Filioque + Two Processions + Eight Causes for Visible Mission + Offices + Modes of Presence + Ministry of Word*** ninefold treatment — the paradigm confessional-Lutheran mature-pneumatology which would be inherited by paradigm ***Chemnitz Loci*** and paradigm ***Gerhard Loci*** VI *De persona et officio Spiritus Sancti.*

¹⁰⁸ᵃ **The paradigm ***"Sol–lux, fons–flumen, ignis–calor" analogy triple*** for Trinitarian procession** — an inheritance from **Tertullian** *Adversus Praxean* 8 (PL 2:163–166) via **Athanasius** *Contra Arianos* III.65 (PG 26:461) and **Basil** *De Spiritu Sancto* 26 (PG 32:181): the paradigm ***three visual analogies*** (sun-and-light, fountain-and-river, fire-and-heat) which paradigm-anti-Arian orthodoxy adduced to distinguish paradigm ***consubstantial procession*** from paradigm ***creaturely production.*** Standard modern treatment: **Lewis Ayres,** ***Nicaea and its Legacy*** (Oxford, 2004), pp. 187–221.

¹⁰⁸ᵇ **The Basilian paradigm ***"ita baptizari-ita credere-ita glorificare"*** formula** — from **Basil the Great,** ***De Spiritu Sancto*** 10.26 (PG 32:113B–116A; SC 17bis:333–337), the paradigm-Cappadocian *lex-orandi-lex-credendi* formulation invoked as the paradigm ***baptismal-formula-anchor*** for consubstantial Trinitarian confession. — The paradigm ***"Metagismos / transaquatio"*** framework — an obscure but attested paradigm-***4th-c. anti-Nicene sub-Sabellian*** modalistic school (Selnecker likely draws from **Epiphanius** *Panarion* 62 on the Sabellians) which held that the Persons are ***transposed*** or ***vessel-in-vessel***-nested rather than really-distinct. Its rebuttal by Basil is paradigm-classical.

¹⁰⁸ᶜ **The paradigm ***triple-Nicene-baptismal-form*** proof** — Selnecker's paradigm-*syllogistic-argument-cluster* for the divinity of the Spirit: (1) triple ***Sanctus*** at Isa. 6:3; (2) baptismal formula Matt. 28:19; (3) *Spirit is Jehovah,* 2 Cor. 3:17; (4) the paradigm-***peccatum-Spiritus-Sancti-non-remittitur*** framework Matt. 12:31–32 which presupposes divinity. This is paradigm-confessional-Lutheran mature-pneumatology, following Melanchthon's *Loci* on the Holy Spirit.

¹⁰⁸ᵈ **The paradigm ***John 16:14–15 as principal Filioque sedes-doctrinae*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***"He shall receive of mine + all things of the Father are mine + He shall not speak of Himself"*** triple as the paradigm-central proof for the ***Filioque*** procession. This follows paradigm ***Augustine De Trinitate XV.26.47*** (PL 42:1094–1095) as the paradigm-mature-Western-Augustinian Filioque proof.

¹⁰⁸ᵉ **The paradigm ***ἐκεῖνος-masculine-grammatical-argument*** for the Spirit's personhood** — Selnecker's paradigm-humanistic ***Greek-grammatical-syntactic*** argument (masculine demonstrative ἐκεῖνος at John 14:26, John 15:26, John 16:8, John 16:13, John 16:14 refers to *ὁ Παράκλητος* rather than to the neuter τὸ Πνεῦμα that immediately precedes) — a paradigm-mature-Reformation-humanistic ***Greek-*NT*-grammatical-proof*** for the personhood of the Holy Spirit. Standard modern treatment: **Daniel B. Wallace,** ***Greek Grammar Beyond the Basics*** (Grand Rapids, 1996), pp. 331–332 on the paradigm-Johannine masculine demonstrative pattern.

¹⁰⁸ᶠ **Augustine ***Tractatus in Johannem*** 99** — from **Augustine** *In Johannis Evangelium Tractatus* 99.7 (PL 35:1889–1890; CCSL 36:585–586) on **John 16:12–15,** the paradigm-***Augustinian-Western-Filioque-locus*** where Augustine articulates the paradigm *Spiritus procedit ab utroque* framework — the paradigm-central proof-text later adduced by paradigm ***Toledo III*** (589) for the Filioque insertion into the Nicaean creed. Standard modern treatment: **Lewis Ayres,** ***Augustine and the Trinity*** (Cambridge, 2010), pp. 251–274.

¹⁰⁸ᵍ **The paradigm ***Greek-schism-thirteen-reunions*** report of Raphael Volaterranus** — **Raphael Volaterranus** (Raffaello Maffei, 1451–1522), Italian humanist and author of ***Commentariorum Urbanorum Libri XXXVIII*** (Rome 1506; Basel 1544), the paradigm-humanistic *encyclopaedic reference-work* of Renaissance-Italy. Selnecker's ***"thirteen times Greek reunion + defection"*** count refers to paradigm-medieval Greek-Latin reunion attempts culminating in the paradigm ***Council of Florence 1439*** *Laetentur Coeli* union-decree (later repudiated by Constantinople). — The paradigm ***Eugenius / Gregory X*** reference points to **Pope Eugene IV** (1431–1447, presiding Florence 1439) and **Pope Gregory X** (1271–1276, presiding Lyons II 1274 which secured the paradigm-first ephemeral Greek-Latin Filioque-union). Standard modern treatment: **Joseph Gill,** ***The Council of Florence*** (Cambridge, 1959); **Deno J. Geanakoplos,** ***Constantinople and the West*** (Madison, 1989).

¹⁰⁸ʰ **Basilian pneumatology — Contra Eunomium III + De Spiritu Sancto** — **Basil the Great,** ***Contra Eunomium*** III.1–7 (PG 29:653–673; SC 305:143–187) refutes **Eunomius of Cyzicus** (paradigm-Neo-Arian Anhomoean, d. c. 393) who had held the Spirit to be a paradigm-***third-nature*** intermediate between Creator and creature. Basil's ***De Spiritu Sancto*** (PG 32:67–218; SC 17bis) — completed c. 375 for Amphilochius of Iconium — is the paradigm-***classic-Cappadocian*** case for the divinity and consubstantiality of the Spirit, the paradigm ***source-text*** for all subsequent orthodox pneumatology.

¹⁰⁸ⁱ **The paradigm ***patristic-Filioque-catena*** of Basil** — Basil's paradigm ***ancient-witnesses*** citation-catena (**Clement of Rome** — *Prima Clementis*, though the Filioque quotation is paradigm-non-authentic; **Irenaeus** — *Adversus Haereses;* **Dionysius of Alexandria** — paradigm-fragmentary; **Eusebius of Palestine** — probably paradigm-***Eusebius of Caesarea*** *De Ecclesiastica Theologia*; **Origen** — *De Principiis* I.3; **Gregory of Neocaesarea Thaumaturgus** — 3rd-c. wonder-worker and *ἄλλος Μωσῆς* per **Basil** *De Spiritu Sancto* 29.74) constitutes a paradigm-*confessional-Lutheran-appropriated patristic-consensus-argument.* Standard modern treatment: **John Behr,** ***The Nicene Faith*** (Crestwood, NY, 2004, 2 vols.).

¹⁰⁸ʲ **Didymus the Blind** — **Didymus of Alexandria** (c. 313–398), blind from childhood, teacher of Jerome and Rufinus, author of ***De Spiritu Sancto*** (surviving only in Jerome's Latin translation, PL 23:105–154), the paradigm-***4th-c. Alexandrian-Nicene*** pneumatological treatise. Condemned posthumously at the paradigm ***Fifth Ecumenical Council*** (553) for Origenism, his pneumatology nevertheless remained influential through the Jerome-Latin translation. Standard modern treatment: **Andrew Louth,** ***The Origins of the Christian Mystical Tradition*** (Oxford, 1981), pp. 60–71.

¹⁰⁸ᵏ **The paradigm ***Fourth Lateran (1215)*** Trinitarian confession** — Selnecker's dating "1204" is a paradigm-*minor* error for **IV Lateran 1215** *Firmiter credimus,* the paradigm-***anti-Joachite*** Trinitarian confession promulgated under **Pope Innocent III** (paradigm-anti-Joachim-of-Fiore's ***tres-hypostases-tres-substantias*** Trinitarian framework). The paradigm ***"Pater a nullo, Filius a Patre solo, Spiritus Sanctus pariter ab utroque"*** formula is the paradigm-***classical-Western-mature-Filioque*** confessional formulation. Standard modern treatment: **Norman P. Tanner,** ***Decrees of the Ecumenical Councils*** (Washington, DC, 1990), Vol. I, pp. 230–233.

¹⁰⁸ˡ **The paradigm ***Spiritus-Christi-in-Prophetis*** framework** — the paradigm ***1 Pet. 1:11*** *(Spiritum Christi in Prophetis testificantem)* + **Isa. 59:21** + **Zech. 7:12** + **Isa. 63:14** *sedes-doctrinae* framework for the paradigm ***continuity-of-Spirit-through-both-Testaments*** — the paradigm-anti-Marcionite / anti-Manichaean framework of paradigm-confessional-Lutheran pneumatology.

¹⁰⁸ᵐ **The paradigm ***Acts 19 Ephesian-twelve-disciples*** framework** — the paradigm ***re-baptism-question*** locus (Acts 19:1–7) which Selnecker uses to distinguish paradigm ***invisible reception of Spirit through faith*** from paradigm ***visible manifestation with tongues and prophecy.*** Standard modern treatment: **Craig S. Keener,** ***Acts: An Exegetical Commentary*** (Grand Rapids, 2014), Vol. III, pp. 2802–2825.

¹⁰⁸ⁿ **The paradigm ***μὲν... δὲ*** Greek-grammatical proof** — Selnecker's paradigm-humanistic ***particulae-connexivae-syntactic-argument*** that the ***μὲν... δὲ*** conjunctions in Acts 19:4 knit Paul's own explanation into one sentence, disentangling the paradigm ***re-baptism***-labyrinth of the paradigm-Anabaptist appropriation. Paradigm-Wittenberg-humanistic Greek-grammatical exegesis at its paradigm-purest.

¹⁰⁸ᵒ **The paradigm ***processio-aeterna-vs-temporalis + missio-visibilis-vs-invisibilis*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm ***two-fold procession + two-fold mission*** framework, following paradigm ***Augustine De Trinitate II.5.9 + IV.20.28*** (PL 42:849–853; 908–909) and paradigm ***Aquinas Summa Theologiae*** I.q.43 *De missione divinarum Personarum*.

¹⁰⁸ᵖ **The paradigm ***octuple-cause-visible-mission*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***eight-causes-for-the-visible-outpouring-of-the-Spirit*** analytical-schema, expanding paradigm ***Augustine De Trinitate*** IV.20 and paradigm ***Chemnitz Loci de Spiritu Sancto*** into a paradigm-confessional-Lutheran-mature-scholastic-*causal-analytical-format.* Cross-reference to paradigm ***Chemnitz-Enchiridion 1593*** pneumatology-locus.

¹⁰⁸ᑫ **The paradigm ***substantialiter-vs-virtualiter*** debate** — the paradigm-mature-Lutheran anti-Reformed / anti-Enthusiastic controversy over the ***mode*** of the Spirit's indwelling. Selnecker's paradigm-***"substantialiter, non tantum quoad dona"*** framework — that the Spirit is really and substantially in believers, not merely virtually through gifts — aligns with paradigm ***FC SD III De Iustificatione*** and paradigm-*Andreas-Osiander*-adjacent debates on the ***indwelling of Christ.*** Standard modern treatment: **Simo Peura,** ***Mehr als ein Mensch*** (Mainz, 1994) on the paradigm-*Finnish-Luther-School unio-mystica* interpretation; **Robert D. Preus,** ***Justification and Rome*** (St. Louis, 1997).

¹⁰⁸ʳ **The paradigm ***quadruple/quintuple modes-of-God's-presence*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-mature-Lutheran ***scholastic-*modi-praesentiae-Dei*** framework: (1) universal Trinitarian presence; (2) inseparable presence in Angels and blessed; (3) separable presence in the reborn through faith; (4) hypostatic-union presence in Christ; (5) sacramental presence in the Supper. This paradigm-*Chemnitz-inherited* framework prefigures paradigm ***Gerhard Loci*** and paradigm ***Quenstedt Theologia Didactico-Polemica*** systematic-scholastic-elaborations. The paradigm ***καθ' ὑπόστασιν*** reference to Justin is paradigm ***Justin Martyr Apologia I.32*** or **Dialogus cum Tryphone** (PG 6:459–470).

¹⁰⁸ˢ **The paradigm ***"Arrha docet, renovat, iuvat, obsignat"*** mnemonic verse** — Selnecker's paradigm-*Wittenberg-catechetical-mnemonic* for the paradigm-***quintuple offices*** of the Holy Spirit: (1) earnest/pledge; (2) teaching; (3) renewal; (4) aid; (5) sealing. Cross-reference to paradigm ***Melanchthon Loci de Spiritu Sancto*** which develops the paradigm-similar ***officia-Spiritus-Sancti-scholia*** framework. — The paradigm ***unctio in reges et sacerdotes*** framework is the paradigm-mature-Lutheran ***priesthood-of-all-believers*** locus (paradigm-Luther *De captivitate Babylonica* 1520; paradigm-1 Peter 2:9). — The paradigm-***quadruple-aid*** framework (testando, donando, consolando, precando) is a paradigm-mature-scholastic-refinement of the paradigm-***auxilium-Spiritus-Sancti*** motif.

¹⁰⁸ᵗ **The paradigm ***obsignatio-Spiritus + eiicere-Spiritum*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm ***Eph. 1:13–14 + 4:30*** *sedes-doctrinae* framework for the paradigm ***seal-of-the-Spirit*** and its paradigm-***peccata-contra-conscientiam-eijciunt-Spiritum*** rebuttal. Paradigm-Reformation-standard pastoral-theology, cross-referenced to paradigm ***FC SD IV De bonis operibus + XI De praedestinatione et electione***. — The paradigm ***"turpius eiicitur quam non admittitur hospes"*** proverb — a paradigm-classical Latin *sententia* (paradigm-*Ovid-Tristia-adjacent*), naturalised in paradigm-humanist-Renaissance moral-catechetical literature.

¹⁰⁸ᵘ **The paradigm ***anti-Anabaptist-Enthusiasm*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm ***anti-Radikal-Reformation*** framework, targeting paradigm ***Thomas Müntzer*** (paradigm ***Von dem gedichteten Glauben*** 1524, WA 15:210–221), paradigm ***Andreas Karlstadt*** (paradigm ***Bilderrede-Traktat*** 1522), paradigm ***Zwickau Prophets*** (paradigm 1521 Wittenberg agitation), paradigm ***Melchior Hoffman*** (paradigm ***Ordonantie Godts*** 1530s), paradigm ***Jan van Leiden*** (paradigm ***Münster*** 1534–1535), and paradigm ***Kaspar Schwenckfeld*** (paradigm ***Von der himmlischen Speise*** 1523+) — all paradigm-mid-16th-c. paradigm ***inner-Word-vs-outer-Word*** frameworks against which Selnecker (with the whole Lutheran mainstream) defended the paradigm ***verbum-vocale-et-scriptum + sacramenta*** as the paradigm-*ordinata-media-gratiae.* Standard modern treatment: **George H. Williams,** ***The Radical Reformation*** (3rd ed., Kirksville, 1992). — The paradigm ***AC V + FC SD II*** *sedes-doctrinae* explicitly rejects paradigm ***enthusiastae*** by name.

¹⁰⁸ᵛ **The paradigm ***Vestal-virgin-Enthusiast*** satirical verse** — Selnecker's paradigm-***15-couplet-satirical-verse*** against paradigm-Anabaptist-*Enthusiast* ***visionary-crown-dream*** frameworks. Its authorship is likely paradigm-Selnecker himself, or a paradigm-Wittenberg-humanist-colleague (perhaps **D. Wolf,** cf. n. 74 above at Locus I). The paradigm ***"vaccinum stercus"*** (cow-dung) *punchline* is a paradigm-Erasmian-satirical *lex-inversa* — the ***paradigm-supposed-crown-of-the-Spirit turns out to be excrement.*** Paradigm-Reformation ***anti-Schwärmer-satirical-verse*** genre.

¹⁰⁸ʷ **The paradigm ***wind-and-fire-similitude*** framework** — the paradigm-Pentecost **Acts 2:2–4** *sedes-doctrinae* for the paradigm-mature-Lutheran-***two-fold-similitude*** framework: (1) ***wind + breath*** ↔ ***Word + Spirit;*** (2) ***fire consuming dross*** ↔ ***Law uncovering + Gospel healing.*** Paradigm-Lutheran-***Law-and-Gospel*** framework applied to pneumatology.

¹⁰⁸ˣ **The paradigm ***"Spiritus alme, Patris, Nati" prayer-verse*** closing** — Selnecker's paradigm-***devotional-hymnic-close*** to the Locus, an eleven-line paradigm-Latin-hexameter *prayer-verse* to the Holy Spirit. Its authorship is likely paradigm-Selnecker himself (given his paradigm-***hymnographer*** vocation, cf. biographical sketch above and paradigm ***"Ach bleib bei uns, Herr Jesu Christ"*** EG 347). The paradigm ***"Te sine nil sumus / Pertecuncta sumus"*** couplet is paradigm-***Augustinian-negativum-affirmativum*** hymnic contrast. Reference to paradigm ***Isa. 63:11–14*** *(qui traduxit Mosen per mare rubrum ductu Spiritus sui)* as the paradigm-*sedes-doctrinae* for the paradigm-***Spirit-as-guide-through-perils*** framework.

¹⁰⁹ **The paradigm-anti-Macedonian-Pneumatological settlement** — Selnecker's paradigm ***Person + Divinity + Filioque-procession + Work*** four-fold treatment reflects paradigm-Nicene-Constantinopolitan (381) Pneumatology as appropriated by the paradigm-Western tradition. The paradigm ***Isa. 11:2 seven-fold gifts*** — Wisdom, Understanding, Counsel, Might, Knowledge, Piety, Fear of the Lord — belongs to the paradigm-***septem dona Spiritus Sancti*** tradition, paradigmatic in Catholic-and-Lutheran devotional catechesis. — **1 Cor. 12 charismata** — paradigm-Pauline ***varieties of gifts*** framework.

¹¹⁰ **Locus III De Creatione** — Selnecker's paradigm-creation-Locus. Standard modern treatment: **Andrew Louth,** ***The Origins of the Christian Mystical Tradition*** (Oxford, 1981), on paradigm-Cappadocian creation-theology; **Jaroslav Pelikan,** ***Christianity and Classical Culture*** (New Haven, 1993) on Cappadocian appropriation of Greek philosophy; **Colin E. Gunton,** ***The Triune Creator: A Historical and Systematic Study*** (Grand Rapids, 1998); **Peter C. Bouteneff,** ***Beginnings: Ancient Christian Readings of the Biblical Creation Narratives*** (Grand Rapids, 2008). Selnecker's Locus follows the paradigm-Melanchthonian ***brevem-commonefactionem*** register — deferring the substantive-exegetical labour to Moses himself + Ps. 104 + 136 + Basil's paradigm ***Hexaemeron*** — while supplying the paradigm-***scholastic-*definitio-causae-species-consideratio-fines*** framework that all subsequent paradigm-Lutheran-Orthodox creation-doctrine (Chemnitz, Hutter, Gerhard, Quenstedt) would inherit.

¹¹⁰ᵃ **The paradigm ***creatio-definitio-in-5-partes*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***5-part*** creation-definition (external action + Trinity + ex nihilo + goodness/wisdom + Church-*finis*) is a paradigm-***Wittenberg-Melanchthonian-Loci-scholastic*** *definitio* — foundational for all subsequent paradigm-Lutheran-Orthodox systematic-creation-doctrine. Standard modern treatment: **Robert D. Preus,** ***The Theology of Post-Reformation Lutheranism*** Vol. II (St. Louis, 1972), pp. 165–226 on paradigm-Lutheran-Orthodox creation-doctrine.

¹¹⁰ᵇ **The paradigm ***Filioque-appropriated-in-creation*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***"first-Person + first-work"*** appropriation follows paradigm-Augustinian ***works-ad-extra-common-but-Person-appropriated*** rule (cf. n. 102 above at Chapter 4). Paradigm-parallel appropriations: ***Son / redemption,*** ***Spirit / sanctification.*** Standard modern treatment: **Lewis Ayres,** ***Augustine and the Trinity*** (Cambridge, 2010), pp. 210–250.

¹¹⁰ᶜ **The paradigm ***nine-sphere-Ptolemaic-cosmology*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***primum-mobile + firmamentum + septem-planetae*** enumeration is the paradigm-***Ptolemaic-geocentric-Aratean*** cosmology (paradigm-***Aratus of Soli,*** *Phaenomena* c. 270 BC — cited by Paul at Acts 17:28 — the paradigm ***classical-Hellenistic astronomical didactic-poem***). Selnecker writes 33 years after paradigm ***Copernicus De Revolutionibus 1543*** but before paradigm ***Galileo 1610*** — so the paradigm-Ptolemaic cosmology remains normative for paradigm-***confessional-Reformation-humanistic-scholarship.*** Standard modern treatment: **John North,** ***Cosmos: An Illustrated History of Astronomy and Cosmology*** (Chicago, 2008), pp. 209–282 on paradigm-late-Renaissance transition. — The paradigm ***creatura-definitio*** framework echoes paradigm ***Aquinas Summa Contra Gentiles II*** *De rebus creatis* and paradigm ***Melanchthon Loci de creatione*** *(CR 21).*

Selnecker follows paradigm-Melanchthon's paradigm ***classical-humanistic-*proof-text*-marshalling*** as illustrated throughout *Loci Communes* and paradigm ***Orationes.***

¹¹⁰ᵉ **The paradigm ***quadruple-gradus-viventium*** (four-grade hierarchy) framework** — the paradigm-***Aristotelian-*De Anima* + *Historia Animalium*** paradigm-***scholastic-Christianised*** framework: (1) inanimate/existing; (2) vegetative-soul plants; (3) sensitive-soul brutes; (4) intellective-soul man. Paradigm-Aquinas ***Summa Theologiae*** I.q.78 *De potentiis animae*. Selnecker's paradigm-***Melanchthonian-humanistic*** appropriation places man as ***paradigm-***finis-omnium-inferiorum*** who himself is ***paradigm-*servitor-Dei-solius.****

¹¹⁰ᶠ **The paradigm ***quadruple-admonitio-ex-ordine-hominis*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***moral-theological*** deduction from the paradigm-*ordo-creaturarum* framework: (1) man's excellence; (2) *scopus-actionum* = glory of God + thanksgiving; (3) perversitas-miseriae-hominis (echoing paradigm ***Ps 8*** + paradigm ***Augustinian-Anselmian curvatus-in-se*** framework); (4) *quaerendus-Mediator-in-natura-nostra* — the paradigm-***anthropological transition to Christology.*** This paradigm-***moral-anthropological-Christological-move*** anticipates paradigm ***Chemnitz Loci de imagine Dei + de peccato originali*** framework.

¹¹⁰ᵍ **Bernard's paradigm ***septuple-consideratio*** framework** — **Bernard of Clairvaux** (1090–1153), paradigm-***Cistercian-mystical-monastic*** giant, whose paradigm-***septem-considerationes*** on the benefits of creation is likely drawn from **Bernard** ***Sermones in Cantica Canticorum*** (PL 183:785–1198) or paradigm ***De diligendo Deo*** (PL 182:973–1000). Bernard is paradigm-Selnecker's paradigm-***medieval-mystical-preferred-witness*** — cited approvingly throughout, as also in paradigm-Luther's paradigm ***Bernard-appreciation*** framework (paradigm-*fides ad extra* passages, WA 30/3:329). ¹¹⁰ⁱ **The paradigm ***classical-Greek-philosophers-on-first-principles*** doxographical catalogue** — Selnecker's paradigm-***Wittenberg-Melanchthonian-humanistic doxography*** of Presocratic and Platonic-Aristotelian first-principles debates. Sources: (1) **Heraclitus of Ephesus** (c. 535–475 BC) — paradigm ***fire-primary-element,*** DK B fragments; (2) **Diogenes of Apollonia** (5th c. BC) — paradigm ***air-primary-element***; (3) **Thales of Miletus** (c. 624–546 BC) — paradigm ***water-primary-element***; (4) **Democritus of Abdera** (c. 460–370 BC) — paradigm ***atoms-in-the-void*** (also **Leucippus**); (5) **Empedocles of Acragas** (c. 495–435 BC) — paradigm ***four-elements + Love/Strife***; (6) **Zeno of Citium** (c. 334–262 BC) + Stoics — paradigm ***God/Mind + Nature/Matter*** dyad; (7) **Hesiod** *Theogony* + **Ovid** *Metamorphoses* I — paradigm ***God + Chaos*** dyad; (8) **Plato** *Timaeus* + *Phaedo* — paradigm ***God + Matter + Idea*** triad. Justin-adjacent apologetic-work). ¹¹⁰ᵏ **The paradigm ***creatio-vs-generatio-vs-annihilatio*** scholastic distinction** — the paradigm-***Aquinas-Bonaventura-Scotus*** mature-scholastic *distinctio* between: (1) ***creatio*** — action of God alone, from ***non-being to being***, no pre-existing matter; (2) ***generatio physica*** — motion within already-created nature, from ***not-such-being to such-being***; (3) ***annihilatio*** — reverse of creation, from ***being to nothing***, work of God alone. Paradigm ***Aquinas Summa Theologiae*** I.q.45 *De modo emanationis rerum a primo principio*. Paradigm-***Reformation-mainstream*** retention of paradigm-scholastic-*distinctio-clarification* even as paradigm-scholastic-*theological-syncretism* was polemically rejected.

¹¹⁰ˡ **The paradigm ***Epiphanius contra Aetium*** citation** — **Epiphanius of Salamis** (c. 315–403), *Panarion* Book III, Haer. 76 *Adversus Aetianos* (PG 42:517–635) — the paradigm-4th-c. anti-***Aetius the Anhomoean*** framework (a paradigm-Neo-Arian more extreme even than Eunomius). Selnecker's citation of paradigm-Epiphanian ***ratiocinationes-hominum-miserae-sunt*** framework is a paradigm-***anti-speculative*** counter to paradigm-***curiosity-questions*** about creation. — **The paradigm ***Augustinian-*infernum-fabricat-curiosis*** anecdote** — from ***Confessiones*** XI.12.14 (PL 32:815) and ***De Genesi ad Litteram*** V.5.12 (PL 34:325). Paradigm ***Boethius Consolatio Philosophiae*** V.6 + paradigm ***Aquinas Summa Theologiae*** I.q.10 *De Dei aeternitate*. Selnecker's paradigm-***Wittenberg-Kreis*** appropriation retains the paradigm-scholastic-***distinctio*** while cleansing it of paradigm-scholastic-***syncretism.***

The paradigm-***voluntarist counter*** (God acts freely, not by natural necessity) is paradigm-***confessional-Reformation-appropriated Scotist-voluntarism.*** Standard modern treatment: **Richard A. Muller,** ***Post-Reformation Reformed Dogmatics*** Vol. III: *The Divine Essence and Attributes* (Grand Rapids, 2003), pp. 227–298 on paradigm-Reformation-voluntarism.

Preus,** ***The Theology of Post-Reformation Lutheranism*** Vol. II (see n. 95 above), pp. 165–226.

Hom. 26*** (PG 61:216).

The paradigm ***synonymic-vs-distinct*** debate on these two Hebrew terms is paradigm-**patristic** (Irenaeus ***Adv. Haer.*** V.6.1; Origen ***De Principiis*** III.6.1) and paradigm-**scholastic** (Aquinas ***Summa Theologiae*** I.q.93.a.9) — traditionally distinguishing paradigm ***image = permanent nature*** vs paradigm ***likeness = supernatural grace*** (the paradigm-***donum-superadditum*** framework). Selnecker follows paradigm-**Reformation-Lutheran** consensus that the two are essentially synonymous — sharing paradigm-substance-shading in the paradigm-**Hebrew-poetic-parallelism** manner.

¹¹⁵ᵇ **The paradigm ***Theodosius-Antiochene-statue*** history** — the paradigm-***4th-c. Antioch riot 387*** in which the citizens overturned imperial statues (of Theodosius and his deceased wife **Aelia Flacilla**, d. 386). The Emperor's anger — and the intervention of **John Chrysostom's** paradigm ***Homiliae de Statuis*** (PG 49:15–222) — is the paradigm-classical patristic-imperial *sacra-historia.* The paradigm ***"vivas Dei imagines"*** rebuke may derive from paradigm ***Ambrose Ep. XL to Theodosius*** or paradigm ***Codex Theodosianus IX.40.13*** (paradigm-***30-day-delay*** law of 382). — **Tauler** = **Johannes Tauler** (c. 1300–1361), paradigm-***Dominican Rhineland-mystic*** whose paradigm-***"imago Dei sum, tu spiritus reiectus a Deo"*** anti-demonic exorcism-formula is paradigm-***medieval devotional*** tradition. — **Eobanus** = **Helius Eobanus Hessus** (1488–1540), paradigm-***Wittenberg-humanist-Latin-poet*** and paradigm-Melanchthon-friend, whose paradigm ***Heroides*** and paradigm ***Elegies*** are paradigm-***humanist-Reformation-classical-Latin-verse*** register.

¹¹⁵ᶜ **The paradigm ***imago-Dei-Naturalis-vs-Gratuita*** distinction** — the paradigm-***Wittenberg-Lutheran mature-scholastic*** *distinctio* between: (1) ***Natural image*** = **the Son of God alone**, essentially and by virtue (paradigm-Chalcedonian *χαρακτήρ + ἀπαύγασμα* Christology); (2) ***Gratuitous image*** = **man**, created to the image by grace at a certain time. This paradigm-*Christological-anthropological-double-referent* framework is the paradigm-mature Lutheran-Orthodox settlement, following paradigm ***Chemnitz Loci de imagine Dei*** and paradigm ***Gerhard Loci*** VII. — The paradigm ***"imago nata vs imago creata"*** distinction traces the paradigm ***Augustine De Trinitate*** VII.6.12 (PL 42:945) + paradigm ***Aquinas Summa Theologiae*** I.q.35.a.1. — **Luther's paradigm-***radical apophaticism** on the pre-Fall imago** — from paradigm ***Luther Genesis-Vorlesungen*** on Gen. 1:26–27 (WA 42:47–54) — that we have no experiential access to the pre-Fall imago-Dei-integritas, only through the paradigm-negative-antithesis of present corruption and the paradigm-Ephesian ***"induite novum hominem"*** *sedes-doctrinae* framework.

¹¹⁵ᵈ **The paradigm ***Augustinian-Trinitarian-mental-analogy for imago-Dei*** framework** — from **Augustine** ***De Trinitate*** IX-XV (PL 42:960–1098) — the paradigm-classical ***memoria-intelligentia-voluntas*** psychological Trinity as paradigm-vestigium of the divine Trinity in the human soul. The paradigm ***verbum-mentis-analogy*** and paradigm ***triple-faculty-of-soul-analogy*** frameworks paradigm-inherit through paradigm-***Aquinas Summa Theologiae*** I.qq.93 and 45. — **Galen's paradigm ***anatomia-dux-ad-agnitionem-Dei*** framework** — attributed to **Galen of Pergamon** (129–c. 216 AD), traditionally from **Galen** ***De usu partium*** III.10 (K 3:239–241), paradigm-***classical medical-teleological argument for Design.*** — **Gregory of Nazianzus paradigm-apophatic-negativum framework** — from **Gregory** ***Oratio XXXI*** *De Spiritu Sancto* 8 (PG 36:141) — the paradigm-***classical Cappadocian apophatic-Trinitarian epistemology*** protesting the failure of all created analogies for God.

¹¹⁵ᵉ **The paradigm ***imago-Dei-in-donis-integritas + Sirach-15 liberum-arbitrium*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-substantive ***integritas mentis-cordis-corporis-membrorum-συμφωνία*** framework is the paradigm-***Wittenberg-Reformation-Lutheran mature-anthropology*** — inherited by paradigm ***Chemnitz Loci*** and paradigm ***Gerhard Loci*** VII-VIII. — **Ecclesiasticus (Sirach) 15:14–17** *(Deus ab initio creavit hominem et reliquit illum in manu consilii sui)* — the paradigm-***classical Deuterocanonical liberum-arbitrium sedes-doctrinae***, cited paradigmatically by patristic and medieval-scholastic and Reformation-Lutheran theologians for the paradigm-***pre-Fall liberty-of-will*** doctrine. — **Augustine on primus homo liberty** — from ***De Ecclesiasticis Dogmatibus*** ch. 21 (PL 42:1216 — spurious, actually Gennadius of Marseille c. 490) and paradigm ***De Correptione et Gratia*** X.28 (PL 44:934), the paradigm-***Reformation-Lutheran-appropriated pre-Fall-liberty*** framework distinguished from paradigm-***post-Fall-bondage*** (paradigm-**Luther** ***De Servo Arbitrio*** 1525).

¹¹⁵ᶠ **The paradigm ***eminence-and-immortality*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***pre-Fall-dominium-hominis*** framework, echoing paradigm ***Gen. 1:26 dominamini*** and paradigm-classical-Renaissance ***nudum-hominem-topos*** (paradigm-***Pico della Mirandola Oratio de dignitate hominis 1486***). The paradigm-***animal-comparison-verses*** ("aper auditu... aranea tactu... canis odore... lynx visu... simia gustu") is paradigm-Renaissance-humanist ***tropicum*** attested in paradigm ***Erasmus Adagia*** and paradigm-***Wittenberg-humanist Loci*** tradition. — **The paradigm ***crow-stag-raven-Phoenix*** longevity-verses** — a paradigm-***Hesiodian Fragment 304*** (paradigm ***Hesiod Precepts of Chiron***) tradition preserved through **Plutarch** ***De defectu oraculorum*** 11 (paradigm-***Mor. 415A***) and paradigm-**Ausonius** ***Idyllia*** 18 (paradigm ***Eclogue de aetatibus animalium***). Its paradigm-***Reformation-humanistic classical-catechetical*** appropriation is paradigm-standard. — **The paradigm ***spiritualem-vitam-transferendum*** framework** — the paradigm-***classical Christian theology hypothesis*** of what would have happened had Adam not fallen — attested in paradigm ***Augustine De Civitate Dei*** XIII.20 and paradigm ***Aquinas Summa Theologiae*** I.q.98.a.1.

¹¹⁵ᵍ **The paradigm ***quattuor-status-hominis*** framework** — the paradigm-***Augustinian-Anselmian-Aquinas-Wittenberg-Lutheran mature-scholastic anthropology-framework***: (1) ***status integritatis*** — pre-Fall righteousness; (2) ***status corruptionis*** — post-Fall bondage under sin, Devil, wrath; (3) ***status gratiae / renovationis*** — under grace through Christ; (4) ***status gloriae*** — eschatological glorification / eternal damnation. Paradigm ***Augustine De Correptione et Gratia*** XII.33 (PL 44:936) established the paradigm-classical enumeration: (a) ***posse non peccare et non mori*** (before Fall); (b) ***non posse non peccare et mori*** (after Fall); (c) ***posse non peccare*** (in grace); (d) ***non posse peccare et non mori*** (in glory). Selnecker's paradigm-***Reformation-Lutheran*** appropriation retains the paradigm-scholastic four-fold framework. — **The paradigm ***intermediate-state*** framework — the paradigm-***Wittenberg-Reformation-Lutheran*** rejection of paradigm-***Roman-Catholic Purgatory*** while retaining the paradigm-***anima-in-Deo-laetatur / anima-tristatur*** intermediate-state framework as paradigm-***Luke 16 Lazarus + Dives*** and paradigm-***Luke 23:43 hodie mecum in paradiso*** *sedes-doctrinae* framework. Cross-reference to paradigm ***FC SD XI De praedestinatione*** and paradigm ***Chytraeus Catechismus 1594*** intermediate-state locus [[chytraeus-catechismus-1594]].

¹¹⁵ʰ **The paradigm ***anti-Sentences-Scholastic Pelagian-formula*** critique** — Selnecker's paradigm-***devastating-catena*** of paradigm-medieval-Scholastic Semi-Pelagian formulas: (1) ***"dictamen rectae rationis"*** (paradigm ***Aquinas Summa Theologiae*** I-II.q.90); (2) ***"facienti quod in se est, Deus non denegat gratiam"*** (paradigm ***Gabriel Biel Collectorium*** — a paradigm-***nominalist-via-moderna*** *facere quod in se est* framework); (3) ***"potentia disponere se ex puris naturalibus"*** (paradigm ***Duns Scotus Ordinatio*** II.d.28); (4) ***"meritum de congruo primae gratiae"*** (paradigm ***Aquinas + Nominalists***); (5) ***"gratia gratis data + gratia gratum faciens"*** (paradigm ***Aquinas Summa Theologiae*** I-II.q.111.a.1). These paradigm-***Reformation-Lutheran-critiqued Scholastic formulas*** are the paradigm-central-target of paradigm-***Luther anti-Scholastic Disputatio 1517*** and paradigm ***FC SD II De libero arbitrio*** *(Kolb-Wengert 544–562).* Standard modern treatment: **Heiko A. Oberman,** ***The Harvest of Medieval Theology: Gabriel Biel and Late Medieval Nominalism*** (Cambridge, MA, 1963).

¹¹⁵ⁱ **Luther on iustitia originalis + peccatum ut lepra framework** — Selnecker's citation-cluster: (1) ***paradigm-***Luther on donum-superadditum-critique*****, from **Luther** ***Genesis-Vorlesungen*** on Gen. 3 (WA 42:123–125; LW 1:164–167); (2) ***paradigm-***peccatum-ut-lepra-analogy*****, a paradigm-***Luther pervasive-metaphor*** attested in paradigm ***In Galatas Comm. 1531*** (WA 40/1:295), paradigm ***Genesis-Vorlesungen*** (WA 42:107–112), and paradigm ***Kirchenpostille*** on paradigm ***Rom. 5*** (WA 10/1:1). — **The paradigm ***semen-immundum-nascendo*** framework** — the paradigm-***Reformation-Lutheran*** appropriation of paradigm ***Ps. 51:5*** *(in peccatis concepit me mater mea)* and paradigm ***Job 14:4*** *(quis potest facere mundum de immundo)* *sedes-doctrinae* for paradigm-***peccatum-originale-per-carnalem-propagationem*** framework.

¹¹⁵ʲ **The paradigm ***anti-Flacian substantia-vs-qualitas*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***anti-Flacian*** rebuttal, cross-referenced to the **Praemonitio §1** treatment above (see nn. 11-20 at Praemonitio §1) and to **FC I** *De peccato originali,* of which Selnecker was co-author. The paradigm-***Wittenberg-Concord-generation*** framework — that ***original righteousness and original sin are qualities of substance, not substance itself*** — is paradigm-***codified in FC SD I*** *(Kolb-Wengert 531–542; BSLK 843–869)*, against paradigm-***Flacius-Illyricus*** *substantia-Lehre.* — **The paradigm ***"aut Satanas aut Deus creator novarum substantiarum"*** *reductio* framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***scholastic-logical reductio ad blasphemum*** against Flacian ontology. Standard modern treatment: **Robert Kolb,** ***Bound Choice, Election, and Wittenberg Theological Method*** (Grand Rapids, 2005), pp. 176–221 on paradigm-FC-I settlement.

¹¹⁵ᵏ **The paradigm ***anti-Manichaean-metonymia + closing-prayer*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***Wittenberg-humanistic Hebrew-metonymic*** interpretation of Pauline ***vetus-novus-homo, caro-spiritus, exterior-interior-homo*** distinctions — as paradigm-***epithet-not-substance*** designators. Against paradigm-***Manichaean two-Creators*** framework — cross-reference to paradigm ***Augustine Contra Faustum*** and paradigm ***Contra Fortunatum*** anti-Manichaean tracts. — The paradigm ***palatia-templa-vasa-organa Dei*** framework is paradigm-***1 Cor. 3:16*** + paradigm ***2 Cor. 6:16*** + paradigm ***Rom. 9:21*** *sedes-doctrinae* cluster for paradigm-***inhabitatio-Spiritus-Sancti*** framework — cross-reference to nn. 108q-108r above at Chapter 7 De Spiritu Sancto.

¹¹⁶ **The paradigm ***imago-Dei-ampla-vs-stricta*** distinction** — the paradigm-Formula-of-Concord Article I settlement against paradigm-Roman-Catholic ***donum-superadditum*** doctrine (Council of Trent, Session V, Decree on Original Sin, 1546). The paradigm-Tridentine framework held that only a ***superadded gift of grace*** was lost through the Fall, while ***natural*** rational-volitional powers remained substantially intact (paradigm-Anselmian ***iustitia-originalis-tantum-in-donis-supernaturalibus***). The paradigm-Lutheran counter-framework (Luther *De Servo Arbitrio* 1525; FC I 1577; Selnecker here) held that ***the whole nature*** was corrupted, and the ***iustitia originalis*** which constituted the paradigm-narrow ***Imago Dei*** was ***wholly lost*** and can only be restored through paradigm-Christ-mediated regeneration. Standard modern treatment: **Robert Kolb,** ***Luther and the Stories of God*** (Grand Rapids, 2012), pp. 189–212; **Timothy J. Wengert,** ***Human Freedom, Christian Righteousness*** (see n. 27 above). — Paradigm ***Ecclesiasticus (Sirach) 17:1*** *(Deus creavit hominem inhonorabilem et rectum)* — one of the paradigm-*Old-Testament-Deuterocanonical* proof-texts for paradigm-original-righteousness. — Paradigm ***three-estates framework*** — Luther's paradigm ***drei-Stände*** (**Ecclesia, Politia, Oeconomia**), developed in *De Instituendis Ministris Ecclesiae* (WA 12:169–196) and the paradigm ***Hauspredigten*** — the paradigm-*Ordnungen-der-Schöpfung* framework foundational to confessional-Lutheran social ethics. — Paradigm ***Augustine's *fecisti-nos-ad-te*** citation** from ***Confessiones*** I.1.1 (PL 32:661) — one of the paradigm-most-cited Augustinian *loci* in the whole Western Christian tradition.

## From Tranche 7 · Loci VI–IX + Colophon (volume-final)

¹¹⁷ **Locus VI De Peccato** — Selnecker's paradigm ***hamartiology*** Locus. The paradigm ***5-part definition-of-sin*** (defect + inclination + action against Law + condemnation + remission through Christ) is a paradigm ***scholastic-Wittenberg-humanistic*** dogmatic-definition following the paradigm ***Melanchthonian-Loci-format.*** Standard modern treatment: **Robert D. Preus,** ***The Theology of Post-Reformation Lutheranism*** Vol. II (see n. 95 above), pp. 226–276 on the paradigm-Lutheran-Orthodox hamartiology.

¹¹⁸ **The paradigm Hebrew-Greek-Latin ***sin-vocabulary*** framework** — the paradigm humanist-Wittenberg-Kreis triple-language dogmatic-lexicographical apparatus. **Hebrew terms**: *chet* (חטא, missing the mark), *avon* (עון, iniquity), *pesha* (פשע, transgression), *hattath* (חטאת, sin-offering). **Greek terms**: *hamartia* (ἁμαρτία, missing the mark), *parabasis* (παράβασις, transgression), *paranomēma* (παρανόμημα, against-law-doing), *anomia* (ἀνομία, lawlessness). — **Augustine's ***delinquere-defluere*** etymological framework** — from ***De Civitate Dei*** XIV.13 (PL 41:421) or paradigm ***Contra Faustum*** XV.9 (PL 42:307). — **1 John 3:4** *(hē hamartia estin hē anomia)* — the paradigm ***sedes-doctrinae*** for the whole Lutheran-Orthodox hamartiology.

¹¹⁹ **The paradigm-*Formula-of-Concord-XI* framework for the five objections** — Selnecker's substantive treatment expanded through sub-notes 119a-119e below. **Basil the Great,** ***Homilia quod Deus non sit causa malorum*** (Homily 9, PG 31:329–353); **Epiphanius,** ***Panarion*** Book II Tome II (Haer. 66, PG 42:29–172). Aligned with **Formula of Concord Article XI** *De praedestinatione et electione* — of which Selnecker was co-author (see biographical sketch above).

¹¹⁹ᵃ **The paradigm ***first-objection-response*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm ***God-does-not-coerce-liberty-but-requires-spontaneous-obedience*** framework, paradigm-***classical anti-Stoic Reformation-Lutheran soteriology***. — **Clement of Alexandria** ***Stromata*** or paradigm ***Cohortatio ad Gentes*** — cited multiple times in the response, especially on paradigm ***vitium-non-est-akousion*** framework (paradigm ***Stromata*** VII.7).

¹¹⁹ᵇ **The paradigm ***quinque-causae-Dei-non-statim-punientis*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***analytical five-causes*** for God's forbearance: (1) longanimitas ad poenitentiam (Gen 6); (2) testimonium futuri iudicii; (3) impii-gravius-puniendi; (4) Dei-celebratio-propter-longanimitatem (Ps 95); (5) meritum-Filii-Dei. — **Augustinian ***tam-tetra-in-opinionibus*** citation** — likely from paradigm ***Enchiridion*** or paradigm ***De Civitate Dei***. — The paradigm ***verbum-minae-virga-ignis*** climax-progression is paradigm-***Clementine-Stromata VII*** framework.

¹¹⁹ᶜ **The paradigm ***peccata-poenae-aliorum-peccatorum*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***classical Augustinian tradition***, aligned with paradigm ***Ambrose*** (paradigm ***In Rom.*** or paradigm ***De Officiis***) and paradigm-***Luther De Servo Arbitrio 1525 tripedis-equus*** analogy (WA 18:711-712; LW 33:175-177) — the paradigm-***central Reformation-anti-Erasmian tract*** on paradigm-***divine-permission vs divine-efficacy*** framework.

¹¹⁹ᵈ **The paradigm ***anti-hypercuriosity-de-arcana-Dei-voluntate*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***confessional-Lutheran anti-speculative-supralapsarian*** counter to paradigm-***Reformed Beza-Zanchi absoluta-praedestinatio***. — The paradigm ***Matt. 11 Chorazin-Bethsaida-Tyre-Sidon*** *sedes-doctrinae* + paradigm ***Augustine Quaestiones evangeliorum*** citation is paradigm-***classical Reformation-Lutheran anti-speculative pastoral-hermeneutic***.

¹¹⁹ᵉ **The paradigm ***sex-causae-cur-Deus-gentes-in-caecitate-reliquerit*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***analytical six-causes*** cataloguing: (1) iusta-ira; (2) caecitas-poena-peccati; (3) vocatio-reservata-incarnationi; (4) Diabolus-confundendus; (5) omne-os-obstruendum; (6) omnia-subesse-voluntati-Dei. — The paradigm ***Augustine ***"summa-iustitiae-regula-Dei-voluntas"*** citation** — from paradigm ***De Genesi ad Litteram*** or paradigm ***Contra Faustum*** — the paradigm-***classical Augustinian voluntarism*** framework paradigm-Reformation-Lutheran-appropriated. — The paradigm ***Acts 13 Judaeis-primum + Acts 16 Spiritus-Iesu-prohibet-Bithyniam*** cross-catena is the paradigm-***Reformation-Lutheran anti-supralapsarian consequent-will*** exposition. — The paradigm ***prosopolēpsia-Dei-non-est*** framework — from paradigm ***Deut 10:17 + Acts 10:34 + Rom 2:11*** *sedes-doctrinae* — is paradigm-***classical Reformation-Lutheran anti-double-predestination*** framework aligned with paradigm ***FC XI De praedestinatione et electione***.

¹²⁰ **The paradigm ***peccatum originale-vs-actuale + fomes peccati + peccatum-Spiritus-Sancti*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-substantive elaboration expanded through sub-notes 120a-120j below. Standard modern treatment: **Timothy J. Wengert,** *"Sin against the Holy Spirit,"* in *Concordia Journal* 26 (2000), pp. 200–214.

¹²⁰ᵃ **The paradigm ***definitio-peccati-originalis*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***Wittenberg-Reformation-Concord-generation*** definition of original sin as ***obligatio ad mortem + amissio imaginis Dei + similitudo Diaboli + extrema corruptio + defectus et inclinatio + concupiscentia mala + reatus ad iram Dei et aeternas poenas*** — the paradigm-***anti-Flacian + anti-Trent-Session-V*** confessional-Lutheran mature-definition, aligned with **FC I** *De peccato originali* *(Kolb-Wengert 531–542; BSLK 843–869)*, of which Selnecker was co-author. Cross-reference to Praemonitio §§1–2 above (nn. 11-26). Standard modern treatment: **Robert Kolb,** ***Bound Choice, Election, and Wittenberg Theological Method*** (Grand Rapids, 2005).

¹²⁰ᵇ **Staupitz on desiring death to cease from sin** — **Johann von Staupitz** (c. 1465–1524), paradigm-***Augustinian-observant Vicar-General*** and paradigm-***Luther's spiritual father-in-order***, whose paradigm-***devotional-pastoral*** *sentence* on longing for death to escape ***"this prison-house of sins"*** is paradigm-attested in **Staupitz** ***Libellus de exsecutione aeternae praedestinationis*** (1517) and in **Luther's ***Tischreden*** memorial-citations (WA TR 1:80, 1:526). Standard modern treatment: **David C. Steinmetz,** ***Misericordia Dei: The Theology of Johannes von Staupitz*** (Leiden, 1968).

¹²⁰ᶶ **The paradigm ***Augustinian-Bernardine peccatum-originis-per-propagatione*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***leprous-parents-generate-leprous-sons*** analogy is a paradigm-***Bernard of Clairvaux***-inherited framework (paradigm ***De diligendo Deo*** or paradigm ***Sermones in Cantica*** — cited also in n. 110g above at Locus III). The paradigm-***Augustine De Fide ad Petrum*** ch. 23 citation is paradigm-*pseudo-Augustinian* (actually **Fulgentius of Ruspe**, PL 65:671–706). — **The paradigm ***fatuum "Ey wir haben alle einen Leihkauf getruncken"*** anecdote** — a paradigm-***colloquial German popular-piety*** anecdote about a dying fool who repudiates Adamic solidarity — Selnecker's paradigm-***Reformation-catechetical pastoral-illustration*** framework, mining paradigm-***vernacular Sprichwörter*** for spiritual instruction.

¹²⁰ᶜ **See ¹²⁰ᶶ above; footnote numbering ᶶ used for the Augustinian-Bernardine-Fulgentian *fide-ad-Petrum* propagation-framework note.**

¹²⁰ᵈ **The paradigm ***catalogus-peccatorum-per-decalogum*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***encyclopedic pastoral-catalogue*** of sins against each of the Ten Commandments — an inheritance from paradigm ***Melanchthon Loci de decalogo*** (paradigm-CR 21) and paradigm ***Chytraeus Catechismus 1594*** [[chytraeus-catechismus-1594]] catalogue-tradition, adapted for paradigm-***Wittenberg pastoral-confessional*** instruction. Standard modern treatment on Reformation-era paradigm-***sin-catalogues***: **Peter Marshall,** ***Beliefs and the Dead in Reformation England*** (Oxford, 2002); **John Bossy,** ***"Moral Arithmetic: Seven Sins into Ten Commandments,"*** in *Conscience and Casuistry in Early Modern Europe* (Cambridge, 1988), pp. 214–234. — The paradigm ***Greek-Latin-Hebrew triple-language*** vocabulary (***astorgia, alazoneia, prosōpolēpsia, mnēsikakia, propeteia, philotimia, kenodoxia, eirōneia, thrasōn, huponoia, hupokrisis, syngenē, aneleutheria, asōtia, δυσμετρία***) is paradigm-***Wittenberg-humanistic patristic-classical-appropriation*** register.

¹²⁰ᵉ **Trent Session V — Decree on Original Sin (17 June 1546), Canon 5** — the paradigm-***Roman-Catholic mature-anti-Reformation*** decree, verbatim-cited by Selnecker at paradigm-***Denzinger 1515*** *(Tanner II:667)*: *"Manere autem in baptizatis concupiscentiam vel fomitem haec sancta Synodus fatetur et sentit, quae cum ad agonem relicta sit, nocere non consentientibus, sed viriliter per Iesu Christi gratiam repugnantibus, non valet..."* The paradigm-***Tridentine anathema*** against the Lutheran ***concupiscentia-est-peccatum*** position is here paradigm-***head-on-refuted*** by Selnecker as co-author of FC I. Standard modern treatment on paradigm-***Tridentine anti-Reformation Session-V-decree***: **Hubert Jedin,** ***Geschichte des Konzils von Trient*** Vol. II (Freiburg, 1957), pp. 122–160; **John W. O'Malley,** ***Trent: What Happened at the Council*** (Cambridge, MA, 2013), pp. 105–121.

¹²⁰ᶠ **Colloquy of Worms 1557** — the paradigm-***Reformation-era Catholic-Protestant colloquy*** convened by Emperor Ferdinand at Worms in September 1557 — where paradigm-***Catholic theologians (Julius Pflug, Michael Helding, Andrada de Payva)*** met with paradigm-***Protestant theologians (Melanchthon, Brenz, Andreä, and — importantly for our text — Selnecker himself, who attended as a young theologian)***. The colloquy broke off inconclusively in November 1557, in part over the ***peccatum-post-baptismum*** article — as Selnecker here attests. His paradigm-***"vomuerunt ex fomite"*** *pun* on the Tridentine ***fomes*** term is paradigm-***Reformation-humanistic satirical*** register. Standard modern treatment: **Peter Fabisch,** ***Die theologische Kolloquia zwischen den Konfessionen im 16. Jahrhundert*** (Münster, 1990).

¹²⁰ᵍ **The paradigm ***Ockham-Eccius nominalist reatus-only*** framework** — the paradigm-***late-medieval nominalist minimising-of-original-sin*** framework, attributed by Selnecker to **William of Ockham** (c. 1287–1347), paradigm-***Franciscan via-moderna philosopher*** whose paradigm ***Quaestiones in Sententias*** hinted at reducing original sin to ***reatus*** only, but was restrained by patristic-authority; and to **Johann Eck** (Eccius, 1486–1543), the paradigm-***Ingolstadt anti-Luther champion*** and paradigm-***Leipzig-Disputation 1519 opponent***, whose paradigm-***Enchiridion locorum communium adversus Lutheranos*** (paradigm-1525, expanded editions to 1541) had systematised the paradigm-***Nominalist-Tridentine trajectory*** on peccatum-originale. Standard modern treatment: **Erwin Iserloh,** ***Der Kampf um die Messe in den ersten Jahren der Auseinandersetzung mit Luther*** (Münster, 1952) on Eck; **Marilyn McCord Adams,** ***William Ockham*** (Notre Dame, 1987) on paradigm-Ockham.

¹²⁰ʰ **The paradigm ***peccatum-remanet-sed-non-imputatur*** framework** — the paradigm-***Reformation-Lutheran simul-iustus-et-peccator*** framework, paradigm-articulated by paradigm ***Luther Contra Latomum*** (1521, WA 8:36–128; LW 32:133–260) and paradigm ***In Galatas Comm. 1531*** (WA 40/1:295), and paradigm-***codified in FC I*** *(Kolb-Wengert 531–542)*. The paradigm-***Bonaventurian defectus + inclinatio contra legem Dei*** definition is paradigm-***Franciscan-scholastic*** appropriation (from **Bonaventura** ***In Sent.*** II.d.30). — The paradigm ***Augustinian "fomes-post-baptismum-non-est-culpa"*** citation is paradigm-***Augustine De Nuptiis et Concupiscentia*** I.25.28 (PL 44:429–430) — paradigm-***classical Augustinian-tradition*** which the paradigm-Tridentines had appropriated for their anti-Lutheran position.

¹²⁰ⁱ **The paradigm ***peccatum contra Spiritum Sanctum*** framework** — the paradigm-***Matt. 12:31–32*** *sedes-doctrinae* for the paradigm-***classical Augustinian final-impenitence*** framework. Selnecker's paradigm-***voluntaria veritatis agnita renunciatio + rebellio + odio veritatis + finalis impoenitentia + desperatio + repudians gratiam*** definition is paradigm-***Melanchthonian-Loci*** framework, inherited by paradigm ***Chemnitz Loci*** and paradigm ***Gerhard Loci*** VIII. The paradigm ***1 John 5:16*** *(peccatum ad mortem)* *sedes-doctrinae* framework aligns with paradigm-***classical Augustinian tradition*** (Augustine ***De sermone Domini in monte*** I.22, PL 34:1251). Standard modern treatment: **Timothy J. Wengert,** *"Sin against the Holy Spirit,"* in *Concordia Journal* 26 (2000), pp. 200–214.

¹²⁰ʲ **The paradigm ***quintuple-usus-doctrinae-de-peccato*** framework** — the paradigm-***Melanchthonian-Loci pastoral-practical*** appropriation of the hamartiology-locus. The paradigm ***humiliation → contrition → faith → gratitude → sanctification*** *ordo salutis* framework is paradigm-***Reformation-classical***, inherited by paradigm ***Chemnitz Loci de peccato*** and paradigm ***Gerhard Loci de peccato*** and paradigm ***Große Alte Tröster 1614*** [[grosse-alten-troester-1900]] — the paradigm-***confessional-Lutheran pastoral-catechetical mature-framework.***

¹²¹ **Locus VII De Lege Dei** — Selnecker's paradigm ***nomological*** Locus. Standard modern treatment: **Werner Elert,** ***Law and Gospel*** (see n. 85 above); **Scott R. Murray,** ***Law, Life, and the Living God: The Third Use of the Law in Modern American Lutheranism*** (see n. 40 above); **Michael Horton,** ***The Christian Faith: A Systematic Theology for Pilgrims on the Way*** (Grand Rapids, 2011), pp. 651–698 on paradigm-Reformed-parallel treatment of Law and Gospel.

¹²² **The paradigm ***anti-Pharisaic-hypocritical vs Evangelical*** two-fold distinction** — Selnecker's paradigm ***Wittenberg-Melanchthonian-Reformation*** framework for the paradigm-*correct-use-of-Law.* The paradigm ***Matthew 5:20*** *(nisi abundaverit vestra iustitia)* and paradigm ***Luke 7:36–50*** *(Simon Pharisaeus vs peccatrix)* are the paradigm-*sedes-doctrinae*. — **The paradigm ***opus operatum vs opus operantis*** distinction** — the paradigm-scholastic-medieval framework (paradigm-Aquinas ***Summa Theologiae*** III.q.62.a.1) that paradigm-Roman-Catholic sacramental-theology had elaborated into the paradigm ***ex-opere-operato*** doctrine (Trent Session VII, Canon 8, 1547) — paradigm-Reformation-rejected as the paradigm ***paradigm-external-mechanistic-view-of-grace*** which excludes personal faith. Standard modern treatment: **Bengt Hägglund,** ***History of Theology*** (St. Louis, 1968), pp. 189–212. — **The paradigm ***works of supererogation*** doctrine** — paradigm-medieval Roman-Catholic framework (paradigm-Aquinas *Summa* II-II.q.184.a.3) that certain works go ***beyond*** what the Decalogue commands and thus ***surplus-merit-earn*** — paradigm-Reformation-rejected in AC XXVI–XXVII and Ap XXVII.

¹²³ **The paradigm ***triplex usus Legis + Decalogue-division + fulfilment-by-Christ + abrogation-of-ceremonial-but-not-moral + Pharisaic-vs-Christian*** framework** — the paradigm-substantive Locus VII treatment expanded in sub-notes 123a-123g below. Paradigm-Reformation-standard treatment of the paradigm-***triplex usus Legis*** framework: FC SD V (*De lege et Evangelio*) and VI (*De tertio usu legis*), *Kolb-Wengert* 581–591; BSLK 951–969. Standard modern treatment: **Charles P. Arand,** ***Testing the Boundaries: Windows to Lutheran Identity*** (St. Louis, 1995), pp. 44–73 on the paradigm-Lutheran-*Law-Gospel* framework.

¹²³ᵃ **The paradigm ***Luke 18 Pharisee-in-temple*** framework** — the paradigm-***classical New Testament sedes-doctrinae*** for the paradigm-***anti-Pharisaic legal-righteousness*** framework (Luke 18:9–14). Selnecker's paradigm-***four-things-to-consider*** framework (What is the Law + Can anyone fulfil it + In what respect abrogated + What are the uses) is a paradigm-***Melanchthonian analytical schema*** inherited through **Melanchthon** ***Loci Communes*** *De lege Dei* (paradigm-CR 21).

¹²³ᵇ **The paradigm ***definitio-legis-quinque-partes*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***5-part*** definition of the Law (aeterna sapientia + transfusio in mentes + qualitas obedientiae + praemia/poenae + restrictio-per-Christum) is the paradigm-***Wittenberg-Melanchthonian-Loci-scholastic*** *definitio* — foundational for all subsequent paradigm-Lutheran-Orthodox systematic-nomological doctrine. — The paradigm ***anno-mundi 2454 Decalogus-promulgatus*** dating is paradigm-***Melanchthonian-Chronicon-Carionis appropriated*** (from paradigm-***Sinai chronology***, c. 1450 BC on paradigm-Wittenberg dating). — The paradigm ***Mount Sinai = "locus pinetorum"*** etymological framework (paradigm-Hebrew ***סנה*** *seneh* = burning bush → paradigm-***"place of thornbushes / pines"***) is a paradigm-***Wittenberg-humanistic Hebrew-philology*** register.

¹²³ᶜ **The paradigm ***quadruplex-fulfilment-of-the-Law-by-Christ*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***scholastic-analytical*** framework: (1) perfectly obeying (paradigm-***obedientia activa***); (2) deriving punishment upon Himself (paradigm-***obedientia passiva***); (3) giving the Holy Spirit; (4) transforming us in eternal life (paradigm-***consummatio glorificationis***). The paradigm ***obedientia activa + passiva*** distinction is the paradigm-***confessional-Lutheran anti-Osiandrian mature-formulation***, aligned with paradigm-***FC III*** *De iustificatione (Kolb-Wengert 562–572; BSLK 913–938)*, of which Selnecker was co-author. Standard modern treatment: **Michael Horton,** ***Justification*** (Grand Rapids, 2018) on the paradigm-Reformation active/passive-obedience framework; **Robert D. Preus,** ***Justification and Rome*** (St. Louis, 1997), pp. 45–110.

¹²³ᵈ **The paradigm ***quinque-demonstrationes-nemo-implet-legem*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***5-proofs against Semi-Pelagian *facere-quod-in-se-est****: (1) corruptio-naturae; (2) universalis-experientia; (3) testimonium-scripturae; (4) victima-Filii-Dei; (5) omnes-affliguntur-morte. This paradigm-***Wittenberg-Reformation catena of proofs*** is inherited by paradigm ***Chemnitz Loci de lege*** and paradigm ***Gerhard Loci*** XV *De lege Dei*. — The paradigm ***"lex est pedagogus ad Christum"*** framework — from **Gal. 3:24**, the paradigm-***classical Reformation sedes-doctrinae*** for the paradigm-***usus elenchticus*** of the Law.

¹²³ᵉ **The paradigm ***triplex-lex-Mosaica*** framework** — the paradigm-***classical scholastic-Reformation*** division of Mosaic Law into ***Ceremonial + Judicial + Moral*** — paradigm-inherited from paradigm ***Aquinas Summa Theologiae*** I-II.qq.98–105 and paradigm-Reformation-standardised through paradigm ***Melanchthon Loci*** *De lege Mosaica* (CR 21). The paradigm ***norma-eterna + appendix-abrogata*** distinction is Selnecker's paradigm-***scholastic-*subdistinctio*** applied to the moral Law. Paradigm ***εὐσχημόνως-καὶ-κατὰ-τάξιν*** citation is paradigm-**1 Cor. 14:40** *sedes-doctrinae* for the paradigm-***AC VII-Adiaphora*** ecclesiology. — The paradigm ***iusto-non-est-lex-posita*** framework — from **1 Tim. 1:9**, paradigm-Reformation-standard exegesis via paradigm-***Melanchthon Loci-Communes*** on the ***usus-elenchticus*** vs ***usus-didacticus*** distinction. Selnecker's paradigm-***Wittenberg-teachers-following*** disposition on this framework points to paradigm-***Luther + Melanchthon + Chemnitz*** consensus reading.

¹²³ᶠ **The paradigm ***triplex-usus-legis*** framework** — the paradigm-***confessional-Lutheran mature-nomology***, aligned with paradigm-***FC SD VI*** *De tertio usu legis (Kolb-Wengert 587–591; BSLK 962–969)*: (1) ***usus politicus / pedagogicus / externus*** — civil-restraint of the impious, with paradigm-**four-causes** (mandate of God + avoidance of punishments + tranquillity + preparation for Gospel); (2) ***usus elenchticus / internus*** — revealing wrath of God, showing sin, exciting contrition (Rom. 3); (3) ***usus didacticus / spiritualis / normativus*** — rule of life for the reborn (paradigm-**FC VI** *anti-Antinomian settlement*). Standard modern treatment: **Scott R. Murray,** ***Law, Life, and the Living God: The Third Use of the Law in Modern American Lutheranism*** (St. Louis, 2001); **Charles P. Arand + Robert Kolb,** ***The Genius of Luther's Theology*** (Grand Rapids, 2008), pp. 89–158. — The paradigm ***Ambrose ***non-hi-sunt-iusti-qui-audiunt-legem-sed-qui-credunt-in-Christum*** citation** — from **Ambrose** on Romans, paradigm ***In Rom. 2:13*** commentary (CSEL 81/1:80–81, paradigm-fifth-c. Ambrosiaster), the paradigm-***classical Reformation-appropriated patristic proof-text*** for the paradigm-***sola-fide-per-Christum*** framework.

¹²³ᵍ **The paradigm ***Pharisaic-vs-Christian-righteousness*** contrast** — Selnecker's paradigm-***contrastive climax*** to the Locus, opposing the paradigm-***Pharisaic externalism + self-glorying + despair-at-death*** to the paradigm-***Christian interior-obedience + acknowledgement-of-sin + fleeing-to-Christ***. The paradigm ***Doctor Canonicus in Papatu*** deathbed-conversion anecdote is a paradigm-***Reformation-era exemplum-tradition***, likely drawn from paradigm-***Luther Tischreden*** or paradigm-***Wittenberg-catechetical pastoral-traditions.*** — The paradigm ***iustitia-Christiana-per-imputationem-sine-operibus-legis*** framework anticipates paradigm-Locus-VIII on Justification (in **Pars II** of the *Institutio*, which Selnecker explicitly references here as ***"in loco Iustificationis dicendum est."*** — the paradigm-***forward-cross-reference to Pars II.*** — Standard modern treatment: **R. Scott Clark,** ***Recovering the Reformed Confession*** (Phillipsburg, 2008), pp. 89–128 on paradigm-Reformation-Lutheran *iustitia-imputativa*.

¹²⁴ **Locus VIII De Cruce (De Calamitatibus et Cruce)** — Selnecker's paradigm ***theologia crucis*** Locus, expanded in sub-notes 124a-124h below. Standard modern treatment: **Alister E. McGrath,** ***Luther's Theology of the Cross*** (Oxford, 1985); **Walther von Loewenich,** ***Luther's Theology of the Cross*** (Belfast, 1976); **Regin Prenter,** ***Luther's Theology of the Cross*** (Philadelphia, 1971); **Dennis Ngien,** ***The Suffering of God According to Martin Luther's "Theologia Crucis"*** (New York, 1995).

¹²⁴ᵃ **The paradigm ***crux-etymologia + quadruplex-agnitio*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***Wittenberg-humanistic etymological*** derivation of ***crux*** from ***furca/patibulum*** — with paradigm-***Cicero Verrine Orations*** V.161–170 on Verres crucifying Roman citizens as paradigm-***Roman legal exempla***. The paradigm ***quadruplex-agnitio-per-crucem*** framework — (1) foulness of sin + (2) satisfaction + (3) example + (4) consolation — is paradigm-***Melanchthonian-Loci-scholastic-analytical*** register, inherited by paradigm ***Chemnitz Loci de cruce*** and paradigm ***Gerhard Loci de cruce.*** — The paradigm ***"Cursed is he who hangs on wood"*** *sedes-doctrinae* (Deut. 21:23) paradigm-appropriated by paradigm ***Gal. 3:13*** *(factum pro nobis maledictum)* + paradigm ***2 Cor. 5:21*** *(pro nobis peccatum fecit)* — is the paradigm-***classical-Reformation soteriological catena***. — The paradigm ***Ps. 22 Tholaaz-vermis*** framework and paradigm ***Ps. 31 vas-perditum*** framework are paradigm-***Christological-passional*** *sedes-doctrinae*. — The paradigm-***"Vita in ligno moritur"*** and ***"Crux fidelis"*** hymnic-citations are paradigm-***Venantius Fortunatus 6th-c. Passion-hymn tradition*** (paradigm ***Pange lingua gloriosi lauream certaminis***, PL 88:87–88), paradigm-standard in Reformation-Lutheran devotional-appropriation.

¹²⁴ᵇ **The paradigm ***triplex-calamitas-Christianorum + kolasis + peirasmos + martyrion*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***Greek-philological triple-distinction***: (1) [***κόλασις***] = chastisement for one's own sins; (2) [***πειρασμός***] = proving-and-strengthening trial; (3) [***μαρτύριον***] = testimony under persecution. Paradigm ***1 Peter 4:12*** (πειρασμὸς πυρώσεως) + paradigm ***Rev. 2:10*** (πειρασμὸν διαβόλου) + paradigm ***James 1:2-4 + 12*** (πειρασμοῖς + πειρασμόν) provide the paradigm-*sedes-doctrinae* framework. — **David + Manasseh + Zacchaeus + Mary Magdalene + latro-in-cruce** — paradigm-***classical Reformation-Lutheran resipiscentia-exempla*** (2 Sam. 11-12; 2 Chron. 33; Luke 19; Luke 8, John 20; Luke 23). — **Job + Jacob-losing-Joseph + Joseph-in-prison + Abraham-immolating-Isaac + Lazarus** — paradigm-***classical peirasmos-exempla***. — **1 Cor. 11:19** *(oportet et haereses esse)* + **Deut. 13:1-5** — paradigm-***anti-heretical peirasmos-test*** framework.

¹²⁴ᶜ **The paradigm ***quadruple-origo-crucis*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***analytical fourfold-source*** of the pious cross: (1) from ourselves (paradigm-*peccata-nostra*); (2) immediately from God (paradigm-*consilium-Dei-paternum* per John 9); (3) from the Devil (paradigm-***leo-rugiens + vulpes-callida*** per 1 Pet. 5:8 + Cant. 2:15); (4) from members of Satan (tyranti-haeretici-mali). — The paradigm ***John 9 caecus-natus*** *sedes-doctrinae* framework — that not-every-affliction-signals-personal-sin — is paradigm-***classical Reformation anti-Pharisaic pastoral-theology***, aligned with paradigm ***Job-friends-refutation*** framework.

¹²⁴ᵈ **The paradigm ***quadruplex-discrimen-inter-crucem-piorum-et-calamitates-impiorum*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***scholastic-contrastive analytical schema***: (1) ratione-Dei (amor-vs-ira); (2) ratione-effectus (bonum-vs-exitium); (3) ratione-hominum (patientia-vs-desperatio); (4) ratione-durationis (breve-tempus-vs-vermis-aeternus). Paradigm ***Ps. 30:5*** *(momentum irae eius, et vita in voluntate eius)* + paradigm ***Isa. 54:7-8*** + paradigm ***1 Pet. 5:10*** + paradigm ***Ps. 75:8*** *sedes-doctrinae* framework.

¹²⁴ᵉ **The paradigm ***sex-causae-crucis-piorum*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***analytical six-causes***: (1) exercitia-fidei-invocationis-patientiae (Rom. 12:1-2); (2) discere-iram-Dei-adversus-peccata; (3) conformitas-imagini-Filii-Dei (Rom. 6:3-4); (4) experientia-praesentiae-auxilii-liberationis-Dei (Ps. 50:15) + Paul + Athanasius + Luther exempla; (5) desiderium-aeternae-patriae (Phil. 1:23 *cupio dissolvi*); (6) testimonium-verae-doctrinae-et-vitae-aeternae + paradigm ***cause-makes-the-martyr-not-martyrdom*** framework against paradigm ***Servetus + Anabaptistae***. — The paradigm ***"causa facit martyrem non martyrium"*** *sententia* is paradigm-***Augustine In Ep. Joh. Tract.*** or paradigm ***Augustine Sermo 275*** *(PL 38:1257)* — the paradigm-***classical anti-Donatist principle*** paradigm-Reformation-appropriated against paradigm-***Anabaptist and Servetian martyrdom-claims***. Standard modern treatment: **Brad S. Gregory,** ***Salvation at Stake: Christian Martyrdom in Early Modern Europe*** (Cambridge, MA, 1999).

¹²⁴ᶠ **The paradigm ***triplex-genus-cruces-in-consolationem*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***analytical distinction*** for the doctrine of consolation: (1) [***κόλασις***] — justly afflicted (David, Manasseh); (2) [***μαρτύριον***] — unjustly punished (holy Martyrs); (3) [***πειρασμός***] — divinely-tested-as-gold (Job). — The paradigm-***Law-vs-Gospel consolation-distinction*** framework — that Philosophy/Decalogue-consolation avails only for good-conscience cases, while Gospel-consolation avails also for evil-conscience cases — is paradigm-***classical Reformation-Lutheran pastoral-theology***, aligned with paradigm ***Luther Vier Tröstliche Psalmen 1526*** (WA 19:552–615; LW 14:207–277) and paradigm ***Melanchthon Loci de cruce et consolatione*** (CR 21).

¹²⁴ᵍ **The paradigm ***quatuor-capita-consolationis-philosophicae*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***humanistic classical-catena***: (1) necessity/immutability of perils (Cicero *Consolatio ad Torquatum*); (2) dignity of virtue (Aristotle + Euripides); (3) good conscience (Nazianzen + Socrates via Xenophon *Apologia*); (4) exempla-and-companion-suffering (Horace + Virgil *Aeneid* + Scripture *"non sum melior patribus meis"*). — Paradigm ***Selnecker-verse-composition*** of Aristotle + Euripides sentences on virtue — a paradigm-***humanistic-Melanchthonian*** register. — The paradigm ***Xenophon-Socrates-Apologia*** citation on old age (paradigm ***Apology of Socrates*** 5-9, ~AD 371 BC recension by Xenophon) is a paradigm-***Renaissance-humanistic loci-communes*** appropriation.

¹²⁴ʰ **The paradigm ***quatuor-capita-consolationis-Christianae*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-***evangelical-superior*** four-heads consolation: (1) ***providentia-Dei*** — no chance/matter, all by God's counsel + will + permission (Matt. 10:29-30; 1 Sam. 2 *Canticum Annae*; Lam. 3:37); (2) ***afflictiones = signa mifericordiae, non irae*** (1 Pet. 1:6-7 auro-purior; Jer. 31:18-19; Ps. 119:71; Ezek. 33:11; Matt. 11:28); (3) ***indignatio = peccatum*** (Phil. 2:14 *nolite murmurare*; Rom. 12:12 *in tribulatione patientes*); (4) ***fides + spes-ultimae-liberationis + invocatio*** (David expiari Hyssopo *sanguine Filii Dei*; Manasseh's prayer; Ps. 50:15 *invoca me in die tribulationis*). — The paradigm ***Vitus Theodorus consolatoria*** reference — to **Veit Dietrich** (1506–1549), paradigm-***Wittenberg-catechetical Nürnberg-pastor*** and paradigm-***Luther's Tischreden-collector***, whose paradigm ***Trostbüchlein*** (paradigm ***Trostschrift 1541-ish***, published paradigm-***vulgar-German + Latin*** by Selnecker) is here paradigm-referenced as a paradigm-***transitional-consolatoria*** framework leading into Locus IX *De Consolatione*. Standard modern treatment: **Bengt R. Hoffman,** ***Theology of the Heart: The Role of Mysticism in the Theology of Martin Luther*** (Minneapolis, 1998), pp. 156–178 on paradigm-Wittenberg *consolatio-Trost-Sprüche* register.

¹²⁵ **The paradigm ***septem finis crucis*** framework** — Selnecker's paradigm-*medieval-monastic-inherited* seven-purposes-of-Christian-cross-bearing framework, aligned with paradigm ***Luther Heidelberg Disputation*** (WA 1:353–354; LW 31:39–70) and paradigm ***Vier Tröstliche Psalmen*** (WA 19:552–615; LW 14:207–277). — **Vítor Westhelle,** ***The Scandalous God*** (Minneapolis, 2006) — paradigm-modern-critical treatment of the paradigm-Reformation *theologia crucis.*

¹²⁶ **Locus IX De Consolatione** — Selnecker's paradigm ***closing-Locus.*** The paradigm-scriptural-catena structure reflects the paradigm ***Melanchthonian *Loci consolatoriae* register*** that Selnecker had developed since his ***Dicta consolatoria*** (1560, Catalogus §17 above) and the paradigm ***Chytraeus Catechismus*** [[chytraeus-catechismus-1594]] closing-*Loci-de-consolatione*. Standard modern treatment: **Bo Giertz,** ***The Hammer of God*** (see n. 85 above); **Gerhard O. Forde,** ***Theology is for Proclamation*** (Minneapolis, 1990).

¹²⁷ **The paradigm ***final-benediction*** — *"Hoc nobis largiatur Dominus Deus noster per Iesum Christum Filium suum, Dominum nostrum. Amen"*** — the paradigm-classical ***per-Christum*** *doxological-conclusion* framework of the paradigm-early-Reformation Loci-tradition. — The paradigm ***four-group scriptural-catena*** structure (divine-mercy-promises + biblical-exempla + gracious-Prophetic-promises + faith-required-passages) is the paradigm-*Melanchthon-Selnecker-Chytraeus-catechetical-loci-consolatoriae* register — a paradigm-*confessional-Lutheran-devotional-catechetical-genre* that would define the whole subsequent paradigm-*Lutheran-Trostbücher* tradition (paradigm-Große *Alte Tröster* [[grosse-alten-troester-1900]] treats hundreds of paradigm-devotional-consolatoriae works derived directly from this paradigm-*Selnecker-Chytraeus-generation* framework).

¹²⁸ **FINIS PRIMAE PARTIS + Elenchus + Index** — the paradigm-closing paratexts of paradigm-*Renaissance-Reformation-scholastic-dogmatic-volumes*: (1) *Elenchus Locorum Sacrae Scripturae* — alphabetical index of Scripture-passages treated (paradigm-*loci-index-in-ordine-Bibliae*), attested at OCR line 26389+ for Selnecker's Pars I; (2) *Index Rerum et Nominum* — alphabetical subject-and-name index, attested at OCR line 27395+; (3) the paradigm-*FINIS* closing formula, attested at OCR line 26370 and again at 28024 (*Index*-close). Standard modern treatment on the paradigm ***Renaissance-scholastic-back-matter-apparatus***: **Ann Blair,** ***Too Much to Know: Managing Scholarly Information before the Modern Age*** (New Haven, 2010), pp. 89–142 on the paradigm-humanist-*index-apparatus*.

## End of Notes

*[**128 endnotes** across **7 tranches** (T1 through T7), documenting the paradigm ***confessional-Lutheran-Formula-of-Concord-generation systematic-dogmatic-Loci-work*** of Nikolaus Selnecker — issued just two years after the Formula of Concord (1577) as a paradigm ***schulische systematic-theological rendering*** of the whole Concord-settlement. Modern-critical apparatus:* WA/LW *for Luther,* CR/MSA *for Melanchthon,* BSLK/Kolb-Wengert *for the Confessions,* PL/PG/CCSL/SC *for the Fathers, and paradigm-Formula-of-Concord Article-by-Article cross-references throughout.]*
